Q96                  The GDPR is a European Union law, and the CCPA is a California state law. Both give people the right to see, correct, and delete the personal data that companies collect about them.
                         No federal law gives all Americans these rights.
                         Would you support or oppose a federal law giving all Americans these rights?


                                                                                    GENDER                               AGE                                                RACE                          EDUCATION               INCOME                         PARTY ID

                                                                                                                                                                                   Asian/                                     $50k–                                        Ind./
